scripted_gui = {

	Loan_Confirmation_Window = {
		context_type = player_context

		window_name = "Loan_Confirmation"
		properties = {
			country_flag_for_trade_screen = {
				image = "[this_loan_country.GetFlag]"
			}
			root_flag_for_trade_screen = {
				image = "[this_loan_country.GetFlag]"
			}
		}
		visible = {
			check_variable = { show_loan_screen = 1 }
		}
		triggers = {
			increase_loan_term_click_enabled = {
				check_variable = {
					current_index < max_index
				}
			}
			decrease_loan_term_click_enabled = {
				check_variable = {
					current_index > 0
				}
			}
			increase_loan_principal_click_enabled = {
				check_variable = {
					purchase_amount < CurrentMaxBorrowable
				}
			}
			decrease_loan_principal_click_enabled = {
				check_variable = {
					purchase_amount > 0
				}
			}
			confirm_loan_purchase_click_enabled = {
				check_variable = {
					purchase_amount > 0
				}
			}
		}
		effects = {
			confirm_loan_purchase_click = {
				add_to_variable = {
					currentMoney = purchase_amount
				}
				set_variable = {
					loan_amount = potential_loan_total
				}
				add_to_array = {
					debtTotalsArray = loan_amount
				}
				add_to_variable = {
					currentDebt = loan_amount
				}
				add_to_array = {
					debtOwnerArray = global_event_target:this_loan_country
				}
				add_to_array = {
					debtOwnerDomesticStatusArray = bank_name_prompt
				}
				add_to_array = {
					debtInterestArray = potential_loan_interest
				}
				set_variable = {
					debtPayment = potential_loan_monthly_cost
				}
				add_to_array = { debtPaymentArray = debtPayment }
				add_to_variable = { currentDebtPayments = debtPayment }
				loan_set_assigning_borrowable = yes

				#close screen
				set_variable = {
					show_loan_screen = 0
				}
			}
			cancel_button_click = {
				set_variable = {
					show_loan_screen = 0
				}
			}
			increase_loan_term_click = {
				add_to_variable = { current_index = 1 }
				ROOT = {
					calculate_loan_details = yes
				}
			}
			decrease_loan_term_click = {
				add_to_variable = { current_index = -1 }
				ROOT = {
					calculate_loan_details = yes
				}
			}
			increase_loan_principal_click = {
				add_to_variable = {
					purchase_amount = 1
				}
				set_temp_variable = { CurrentMaxBorrowable_temp = CurrentMaxBorrowable }
				add_to_temp_variable = { CurrentMaxBorrowable_temp = 0.5 }
				round_temp_variable = CurrentMaxBorrowable_temp
				clamp_variable = {
					var = purchase_amount
					max = CurrentMaxBorrowable_temp
				}
				ROOT = {
					calculate_loan_details = yes
				}
			}
			increase_loan_principal_shift_click = {
				add_to_variable = {
					purchase_amount = 10
				}
				set_temp_variable = { CurrentMaxBorrowable_temp = CurrentMaxBorrowable }
				add_to_temp_variable = { CurrentMaxBorrowable_temp = 0.5 }
				round_temp_variable = CurrentMaxBorrowable_temp
				clamp_variable = {
					var = purchase_amount
					max = CurrentMaxBorrowable_temp
				}
				ROOT = {
					calculate_loan_details = yes
				}
			}
			decrease_loan_principal_click = {
				add_to_variable = {
					purchase_amount = -1
				}
				ROOT = {
					calculate_loan_details = yes
				}
			}
			decrease_loan_principal_shift_click = {
				add_to_variable = {
					purchase_amount = -10
				}
				clamp_variable = {
					var = purchase_amount
					min = 0
				}
				ROOT = {
					calculate_loan_details = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

            Debts_Button_click = {
                clear_array = currentDebtTotalsArray
                clear_array = currentDebtPaymentArray
                clear_array = currentDebtOwnerArray
                clear_array = currentDebtInterestArray
                for_each_loop = {
                    array = debtOwnerArray
                    index = i
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            check_variable = {
                                debtTotalsArray^i > 0
                            }
                        }
                        add_to_array = { currentDebtTotalsArray = debtTotalsArray^i }
                        add_to_array = { currentDebtPaymentArray = debtPaymentArray^i }
                        add_to_array = { currentDebtOwnerArray = debtOwnerArray^i }
						add_to_array = { currentDebtInterestArray = debtInterestArray^i }
                        #set_variable = {
                        #    interest_rate_array_adj = debtInterestArray^i
                        #}
                        #add_to_variable = {
                        #    interest_rate_array_adj = -1
                        #}
                        #add_to_array = { currentDebtInterestArray = interest_rate_array_adj }
                    }
                }
                set_variable = { show_current_loans = 1 }
                set_variable = { show_current_borrowing = 0 }
                set_variable = { show_current_overview = 0 }
				set_variable = { show_current_fiscal = 0 }
				set_variable = { show_current_employment = 0 }
                set_variable = { show_current_policy = 0 }
            }

            convert_to_petrostate_click = { add_political_power = -60 cwic_action_convert_petrostate = yes }
        }
    }
    Finance_Tab_Current_Loans_Section = {
        context_type = player_context
        parent_scripted_gui = "Finance_tab_menu"
        window_name = "current_loans_grid"
		visible = {
			check_variable = { show_current_loans = 1 }
		}
		properties = {
			country_flag_for_current_loans = {
				image = "[THIS.GetFlag]"
			}
        }
		dynamic_lists = {
			current_debts_listing_grid = {
				array = currentDebtOwnerArray
                change_scope = no
				entry_container = current_debts_listing_entry
			}
			current_debts_listing_flag_grid = {
				array = currentDebtOwnerArray
                change_scope = yes
				entry_container = current_debts_listing_entry_flag
			}
        }
		triggers = {
			pay_off_loan_click_enabled = {
				check_variable = {
					currentMoney > ROOT.currentDebtTotalsArray^i
				}
			}
		}
		effects = {
			pay_off_loan_click = {
				set_variable = {
					ROOT.debtTotalsArray^i = 0
				}
				set_variable = {
					ROOT.debtPaymentArray^i = 0
				}
				set_variable = {
					ROOT.debtOwnerArray^i = 0
				}
				subtract_from_variable = {
					ROOT.currentMoney = currentDebtTotalsArray^i
				}
				subtract_from_variable = {
					ROOT.currentDebt = currentDebtTotalsArray^i
				}
				set_variable = {
					ROOT.debtOwnerDomesticStatusArray^i = 0
				}
				set_variable = {
					ROOT.currentDebtOwnerArray^i = 0
				}
				remove_from_array = {
					array = ROOT.debtTotalsArray
					index = i
				}
				remove_from_array = {
					array = ROOT.debtPaymentArray
					index = i
				}
				remove_from_array = {
					array = ROOT.debtOwnerArray
					index = i
				}
				remove_from_array = {
					array = ROOT.debtInterestArray
					index = i
				}
                clear_array = currentDebtTotalsArray
                clear_array = currentDebtPaymentArray
                clear_array = currentDebtOwnerArray
                clear_array = currentDebtInterestArray
                for_each_loop = {
                    array = debtOwnerArray
                    index = i
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            check_variable = {
                                debtTotalsArray^i > 0
                            }
                        }
                        add_to_array = { currentDebtTotalsArray = debtTotalsArray^i }
                        add_to_array = { currentDebtPaymentArray = debtPaymentArray^i }
                        add_to_array = { currentDebtOwnerArray = debtOwnerArray^i }
						add_to_array = { currentDebtInterestArray = debtInterestArray^i }
                    }
                }

			}
		}
    }
}
scripted_gui = {
	line_graph_sample_scripted_gui = {
		window_name = "line_graph_sample_gui"
		context_type = player_context
        parent_scripted_gui = "Finance_tab_menu"

		effects = {
			close_button_click = {
				clr_country_flag = open_line_gui
			}
			add_button_click = {
				# Invoke scripted effect to add a random point to the line
				# See scripted effect file

				SAMPLE_add_random_point_to_line = yes
			}
		}

		# Set up dynamic list to generate one line segment per point
		dynamic_lists = {
			line_segments = {
				array = GDP_graph_offsets
				change_scope = no
				entry_container = line_graph_line_segment
				index = segment_idx
			}
		}

		properties = {
			segment = {
				# Sets they position of the line segment to the graph offset
				# Equal to -y1
				y = GDP_graph_offsets^segment_idx

				# Tells the shader how steep to draw the line
				# Must be equal to (y2 - y1) / 2 + 50
				frame = GDP_graph_slopes^segment_idx
			}
		}

		triggers = {
			# Optional. This hides every other x label, to give them some breathing room
			label_visible = {
				set_temp_variable = { si = segment_idx }
				modulo_temp_variable = { si = 2 }
				check_variable = { si = 0 }
			}
		}
	}
}

	Foreign_Capital_Picker_electronics_Window = {
		context_type = player_context
		window_name = "Foreign_Capital_Picker_electronics"
		visible = {
			check_variable = { show_fc_picker_electronics = 1 }
		}
		properties = {
			cwic_fc_flag_att_electronics = {
				image = "[USA.GetFlag]"
			}
			cwic_fc_flag_ibm_electronics = {
				image = "[USA.GetFlag]"
			}
			cwic_fc_flag_itt_electronics = {
				image = "[USA.GetFlag]"
			}
			cwic_fc_flag_olivetti_electronics = {
				image = "[ITA.GetFlag]"
			}
			cwic_fc_flag_philips_electronics = {
				image = "[HOL.GetFlag]"
			}
			cwic_fc_flag_rca_electronics = {
				image = "[USA.GetFlag]"
			}
			cwic_fc_flag_siemens_electronics = {
				image = "[WGR.GetFlag]"
			}
			cwic_fc_flag_panasonic_electronics = {
				image = "[JAP.GetFlag]"
			}
			cwic_fc_flag_sony_electronics = {
				image = "[JAP.GetFlag]"
			}
			cwic_fc_flag_toshiba_electronics = {
				image = "[JAP.GetFlag]"
			}
		}
		triggers = {
			Foreign_Capital_Pick_att_electronics_visible = { always = yes }
			cwic_fc_card_att_electronics_visible = { OR = { AND = { cwic_mnc_pick_invite_att_electronics_ok = yes has_political_power > 24 } AND = { cwic_mnc_pick_concession_att_electronics_ok = yes has_political_power > 49 } } }
			cwic_fc_card_dim_att_electronics_visible = { NOT = { OR = { AND = { cwic_mnc_pick_invite_att_electronics_ok = yes has_political_power > 24 } AND = { cwic_mnc_pick_concession_att_electronics_ok = yes has_political_power > 49 } } } }
			cwic_fc_pending_att_electronics_visible = { has_variable = cwic_invite_att_electronics }
			cwic_fc_cooldown_att_electronics_visible = { NOT = { has_variable = cwic_invite_att_electronics } has_country_flag = cwic_invite_cd_att_electronics }
			cwic_fc_full_att_electronics_visible = { NOT = { has_variable = cwic_invite_att_electronics } NOT = { has_country_flag = cwic_invite_cd_att_electronics } has_variable = corp_att_presence_electronics NOT = { check_variable = { corp_att_presence_electronics < 5 } } }
			cwic_fc_invite_att_electronics_visible = { NOT = { has_variable = cwic_invite_att_electronics } NOT = { has_country_flag = cwic_invite_cd_att_electronics } NOT = { has_variable = corp_att_presence_electronics NOT = { check_variable = { corp_att_presence_electronics < 5 } } } cwic_mnc_pick_invite_att_electronics_ok = yes has_political_power > 24 }
			cwic_fc_invite_poor_att_electronics_visible = { NOT = { has_variable = cwic_invite_att_electronics } NOT = { has_country_flag = cwic_invite_cd_att_electronics } NOT = { has_variable = corp_att_presence_electronics NOT = { check_variable = { corp_att_presence_electronics < 5 } } } cwic_mnc_pick_invite_att_electronics_ok = yes NOT = { has_political_power > 24 } }
			cwic_fc_concession_att_electronics_visible = { NOT = { has_variable = cwic_invite_att_electronics } NOT = { has_country_flag = cwic_invite_cd_att_electronics } NOT = { has_variable = corp_att_presence_electronics NOT = { check_variable = { corp_att_presence_electronics < 5 } } } NOT = { cwic_mnc_pick_invite_att_electronics_ok = yes } cwic_mnc_pick_concession_att_electronics_ok = yes has_political_power > 49 }
			cwic_fc_concession_poor_att_electronics_visible = { NOT = { has_variable = cwic_invite_att_electronics } NOT = { has_country_flag = cwic_invite_cd_att_electronics } NOT = { has_variable = corp_att_presence_electronics NOT = { check_variable = { corp_att_presence_electronics < 5 } } } NOT = { cwic_mnc_pick_invite_att_electronics_ok = yes } cwic_mnc_pick_concession_att_electronics_ok = yes NOT = { has_political_power > 49 } }
